Each accepted lesson names the skill or AGENTS.md section it belongs in, where the user can read and diff it; a lesson a check could enforce goes on the Backlog. Changes nothing until the user approves each lesson. Load it for /reflect, and at the end of a session where the user corrected you. ([zgeoff/skills](https://github.com/zgeoff/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reflect))
- **reflect**: Deep reflection on patterns in the event log — analysis only, no modifications ([zhenzhongxu/path-claudecode](https://github.com/zhenzhongxu/path-claudecode/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reflect))
- **Reflect (Mid-Conversation Reassessment)**: Use when the user says "step back", "zoom out", "are we missing something", "sanity check", or a conversation has gone deep without strategic check-in — pauses and honestly reassesses direction, assumptions, and bias.
